**2026-08-10 ~07:38 — Gatefall World Rule Profile 1.40 adopted (`EVT-000247`; no fictional time consumed).**

- **Section 17's boss-kill close is removed.** An instant dungeon now ends on exactly two conditions: the **two-hour mark**, measured from the moment the key is used, or **the Bearer choosing to leave**. Nothing else closes it. The boss kill still settles its core, its boss-drop d100, its kill XP, and the Gate-clear milestone at the instant it lands; the interior then holds for whatever remains of the two hours.
- **Why, and the clear that proved it.** The old rule made a private instance strictly worse than a licensed Gate at the moment it was won — in a public Gate a boss's death *opens* a two-hour clear window (Section 9.7), while here it collapsed the interior instantly. It also worked against Section 17's own loot rule: every instance carries a deposit rolled at opening, extraction costs time, and killing the boss was the one thing a Bearer working that deposit could not afford to do. **Dungeon 1 of the 2026-08-09 run is the demonstration** (`EVT-000232`) — its 22-crystal D-Rank deposit was never extracted and was lost when the boss kill closed the instance.
- **The two hours are absolute.** A boss killed with five minutes left leaves five minutes, not a fresh window. Clearing early is what buys looting time.
- **Two clarifications the old text never settled.** Population is rolled **once at opening** and never respawns — the post-kill remainder is time to mine and walk, not to farm. And closing **returns everyone inside to the world** rather than stranding them, as the Section 8.3 penalty zone already does at four hours; Section 9.7's lost-inside rule is a public-Gate rule that does not reach an instant dungeon.
- **Nothing recomputes, and Dungeon 1's loss stands.** No stored field, magnitude, cost, counter, probability, owned item, completed transaction, or resolved outcome changes. No completed instant dungeon is reopened, re-looted, re-timed, or recomputed — all nine clears on record ended on their boss kills as the rule then required. **The 22 unextracted crystals are not restored, re-rolled, or compensated:** that loss is promoted canon and the engine forbids fix-it-later retcons of it. 1.40 is why it will not recur, not a reversal of the time it did.
- **No key is banked at adoption.** The Armory Key [D-Rank] was consumed opening Dungeon 1 (`EVT-000231`), Dungeon 2's key was bought and spent, and Dungeon 3's boss dropped a rune rather than a key (`EVT-000233`-`EVT-000235`). The first instance to run under 1.40 will open from a key not yet acquired.
- **Versions.** `system_state.profile_version` 1.39 → 1.40. Engine 0.2.0 and Data Model 0.1.5 unchanged. Immutable checkpoints remain byte-unchanged and run the chain through 1.40 at readiness. New migration record `worlds/gatefall/migrations/1.39_to_1.40.md`.
- **Repository allocation.** `EVT-000247`; no entity, record, or relationship identifier minted. New high-water mark **EVT-000247**. Updated live targets: `090`, `100`, `160`, `170`, `180`, and `system/ID_REGISTRY.md`.

**2026-08-10 ~07:38 — Gatefall World Rule Profile 1.41 adopted (`EVT-000248`; no fictional time consumed).**

- **Section 6.1.2's authored variance now reaches the NPCs a campaign actually plays.** Individual pool variance of up to ±10% has existed since Profile 1.32, but its authored class named only `220_NOTABLE_FIGURES.md` and `institutions/` rosters — both world-layer files. Every crewmate, contact, employer, and rival lives in the campaign's own `130_NPCS_AND_FACTIONS.md`, which the rule did not name, so those NPCs belonged to **no class at all**: not authored, not rolled, not excluded. The authored row now names all three sources on identical terms.
- **The audit behind it.** Not one entity in the repository had ever carried a variance — 0 of 14 world figures, 0 across the campaign ledger, 0 rolled in play. All eight `institutions/` directories hold zero Character blocks, so half the 1.32 definition addressed an empty set. The visible cost was Flux Sight: at its C rung it reads an awakened human's Health and Mana, Alexander has held that rung since Perception 44, and it returned the flat table value — exactly what a Rank read already gave him.
- **Two derivation boundaries closed.** **Section 13.5's mender field-touch reads the flat table** — a C-Rank mender's touch is 62 whatever their variance, so a deeper well buys more touches and never a stronger one. **Section 5.2's Mana tiers read the varied maximum** — a +10% C-Rank hunter's Signature ability costs 34 rather than 31, and Section 5.3's running-dry threshold is 25% of that same varied maximum.
- **Section 13.1 gains a recording convention that authors nothing.** Section 5.1 stands unchanged — no creature is authored by the baseline, and no NPC gets a stat block, ability list, or cost sheet. A named NPC's entry may now carry the *derived* form of existing rules: `rank`, `pool_variance`, `pools`, `signature_ability`, `learned_techniques`, `equipment`.
- **Nine variances authored.** Owen Callahan **+10%** (Health 275, Mana 137); Priya Okafor **none** (100 / 50); Julian Boyd **+5%** (42 / 21); Renata Marchetti **−5%** (38 / 19); Ruth **+5%** (262 / 131); on Ironline's trial crew, Reggie Calloway **+5%** (105 / 52), Bhavna Iyer **none**, Foster Nakashima **−5%** (95 / 47), Callie Dunmore **none**. Only NPCs whose Rank is actually established in play received one; no Rank was invented for anyone.
- **Two ledger defects corrected.** Owen's Stone Skin was labelled *Novice mastery*; Section 13.6 gives an NPC learned technique no mastery track, counter, or progression, so 30% stands as the authored D-Rank technique effect and the label is retired. Section 13.6's "no Mana ledger" clause is reconciled with 1.32 rather than changed: the technique creates none, the entity has one, and the technique is paid from it at a tier.
- **Nothing recomputes.** Variance is authored forward. No exchange is recomputed, no fight re-resolved, no injury re-tiered, no Mana spend restated. **No elite or boss is retroactively rolled** — 1.32's mandatory first-contact roll has never once been performed, including across the three D-Rank instant dungeons of 2026-08-09; those instances are closed and their populations discarded, and every fight resolved at flat values, which is a legal band outcome. **Owen's narrated two-thirds Stone Skin cost (`EVT-000180`) is not restated** — it predates 1.32's adoption at `EVT-000210` and was never bound by the tier table.
- **Versions.** `system_state.profile_version` 1.40 → 1.41. Engine 0.2.0 and Data Model 0.1.5 unchanged. Immutable checkpoints remain byte-unchanged and run the chain through 1.41 at readiness. New migration record `worlds/gatefall/migrations/1.40_to_1.41.md`; chain 1.1 → 1.41, 40 edges.

**2026-08-10 ~07:38 — Gatefall World Rule Profile 1.42 adopted, and six signature abilities authored (`EVT-000250`; no fictional time consumed).**

- **Section 13.6 gains a Rank floor.** A signature ability is now required of every named NPC hunter at **D-Rank or above**, not of every named NPC hunter. An E-Rank entry carrying none is correct rather than incomplete.
- **What the floor does not say.** An E-Rank's gift is not absent — it is too slight to name, file, or build a reputation on, which is why the boards record hunters at that Rank by working type and licence number, and why Section 9.4 has most E- and D-Ranks earning as miners and harvesters. Section 13.6 still reads *most* awakened manifest a signature, not all, and an E-Rank's may be named later if the fiction needs it.
- **The Bearer's blank card reads more sharply for it.** A blank card is *nothing at all*, which stays distinct from the slight, unfiled gift an ordinary E-Rank carries — the contrast that made it notorious is preserved rather than eroded.
- **Six authored:** Priya Okafor **Follow-Through** (her mana carries a committed strike past where the body stops — the same gift as the third-beat flaw Alexander diagnosed at `EVT-000116`); Ruth **Warm Hands**; Reggie Calloway **Threshold**; Bhavna Iyer **Tally**; Foster Nakashima **Backstop**; Callie Dunmore **Slip**.
- **One deliberate restraint.** Backstop is a perimeter **sense**, not a barrier, and does **not** trigger Section 13.5's warden effect — Ironline's trial crew gains no formation defensive step from him. None of the six triggers any Section 13.5 effect; Owen's mending remains the only signature in the ledger carrying mechanics.
- **Five E-Rank hunters recorded as correctly carrying none:** Julian Boyd, Renata Marchetti, Denny Osei, Marisol Ruiz, Milo. Each entry states the reasoning rather than leaving a bare empty field.
- **Nothing recomputes.** No authored signature is removed from any entry at any Rank. A signature has never carried magnitude, so authoring six alters no damage, reduction, pool, cost, or party effect; every scene these six appeared in stands as recorded.

**2026-08-10 — Owen Callahan's 2026-08-09 shift length reconciled (owner ruling; live-ledger correction, no new Event, no fictional time).**

- **The defect.** Four live ledgers described Owen's Sunday 2026-08-09 Edgewater shift as **eleven hours**, which no reading of canon supports. Three anchors pin it: he ran the **06:00–07:50** lakefront regimen with Alexander that morning (`EVT-000218`), which forecloses any earlier start; `EVT-000218` states the shift **begins at 08:00**; and he came off it, texted, and reached Alexander's door by **~16:50** (`EVT-000239`, `EVT-000241`), which forecloses a later finish. 08:00 to 16:30 is **eight and a half hours**.
- **The ruling considered and set aside.** A **05:30 start** was proposed, to preserve the eleven hours by treating `EVT-000218`'s 08:00 as his standard rostered start rather than that day's. It is impossible: he was on the lakefront with Alexander from 06:00, and that run is where they committed to living together — the scene `OBJ-15` turns on. The run is not movable, so the start is not movable.
- **Settled:** the shift ran **08:00–16:30**. `EVT-000218`'s 08:00 stands exactly as written and is not amended. The "eleven-hour" phrasing is corrected in `130`, `140`, `160` (`EVT-000239`, `EVT-000241`), `170`, and `180`.
- **Nothing else moves.** No anchor, pool, recovery settlement, transaction, or resolved outcome changes; no scene is reopened. What made the day rough was never its length — a patient coded in bay three at eleven and ate the whole of it — and **Monday 2026-08-10 off is earned by working the weekend at all**, outside his fixed Wednesday–Friday rota, not by the hours worked on the Sunday.
- **The rota itself is unchanged.** Owen's authored week — three twelve-hour shifts, Wednesday to Friday, 08:00–20:00 — stands, and the 2026-08-08/09 weekend remains the exception it was already recorded as.

**2026-08-10 ~07:38 — Gatefall World Rule Profile 1.43 adopted: the elixir given away (`EVT-000251`; no fictional time consumed).**

- **The first exception to Section 2 since the profile was written.** An awakened's capability is their fixed Rank and their pools never move for anything they do — all of that stands. 1.43 adds exactly one hand that reaches past it: **the Bearer's, spending a Section 12.5 stat elixir on another awakened.** The System is not the world, and what the Bearer hands someone is the System's.
- **New Section 12.5.1.** **Vitality and Intelligence only** (Strength/Agility/Perception have nothing to attach to under Section 13.1). **Uncapped for a non-Bearer** — the max-3-lifetime limit binds the Bearer's own progression, not what he spends on someone else. Each point adds flat on top of the recipient's variance-adjusted Rank pool: **+4 max Health per Vitality, +2 max Mana per Intelligence**. No Stat sheet, no level, XP, skill, mastery, or equipment Stat line, and the Section 4.3 domain rating is untouched — the recipient still resolves every contested action at their Rank.
- **Section 14.4 is unmoved, and the recipient still knows.** No window, no notification, no number. But it is their own body rather than a technique arriving in it, and the change registers **immediately** as depth. A mender will know something happened the moment it does and will not be able to say what.
- **Elixir points land outside Section 6.1.2's bracket.** Variance is authored once and never recomputed. The first point past a Rank's ceiling makes the recipient a number **no hunter of that Rank can be** — real instantly, invisible until a Flux Sight read at the C rung, a BGM Ranking Bench instrument, or a Section 19.2 re-assessment measures it.
- **True Rank advances; the card does not.** When both totals reach the next Rank's **flat** Section 6.1 values, true Rank advances and they resolve at it in full. The licence still says what BGM measured at Awakening, so Section 9.4's minimums and every contract ceiling keep reading the card until re-assessment — whose only sanctioned explanation is Section 19.3's **reawakening**, which would be a lie, because nothing was mismeasured. **The state between Ranks has no name**, no procedure, and no precedent, deliberately.
- **`OBJ-18` has a mechanism.** Owen Callahan stands at 275 / 137 — exactly the C-Rank bracket ceiling. **One** Intelligence elixir takes him to **139** for **5,000 g** against Alexander's 13,151. A true B-Rank needs 82 Vitality and 82 Intelligence points: **164 elixirs, 820,000 g.** The promise was never impossible; it was impossible for anyone not carrying a System.
- **`GTF-OVR-002` and Section 2 amended** to admit it — pools never grow *of themselves*, with Section 12.5.1 named as the single intervention that moves them.
- **Nothing has happened.** No elixir has been purchased, transferred, or consumed in any campaign; no pool anywhere is altered. **No past transfer is reread as this rule** — the Stone Skin rune (`EVT-000178`) taught a technique, and the Standard Mana Potion (`EVT-000180`) restored expended capacity without touching capacity itself, which is exactly the distinction `OBJ-18` turns on. The Bearer's own max-3 cap is unchanged.
- **Versions.** `system_state.profile_version` 1.42 → 1.43. Engine 0.2.0 and Data Model 0.1.5 unchanged. New migration record `worlds/gatefall/migrations/1.42_to_1.43.md`; chain 1.1 → 1.43, 42 edges.
- **Repository allocation.** `EVT-000251`; no entity, record, or relationship identifier minted. New high-water mark **EVT-000251**. Updated live targets: `090`, `100`, `140`, `160`, `170`, `180`, `system/ID_REGISTRY.md`, `system/WORLDS_AND_CAMPAIGNS.md`.

- **Standing rule adopted (player instruction).** When `/system shop` is opened and the stored Daily Premium cycle's boundary has already passed, the Runtime rotates one fresh cycle live before rendering the shop — never more than one, regardless of how many boundaries were missed. Over dinner, Alexander told Owen the complete account of the 2026-08-08 Wade Bishop D-Rank trial for the first time — including deliberately withholding an ambush warning to see what the crew would do, and two crewmates walking out untreated. Owen's reaction separated Alexander's own choice (still weighing on him) from Ironline's structural failure (sending an untested, mender-less crew to test a stranger).

Owen fought the Carapaced stone-and-root guardian alone: a natural critical fumble unpunished by the guardian's own catastrophic-failure counter, two partial-success hits, then a natural critical kill — no damage taken. Boss/milestone XP 110: XP 750→860/1300. Walking back, Owen and Alexander worked out that licensed artificers, not just the System shop, can build Owen a longsword or ranged weapon from a banked core; nothing bought yet.

**2026-08-11 ~09:35 — Gatefall World Rule Profile 1.44 adopted: the tracked board becomes dispatchable (`EVT-000268`; no fictional time consumed).**

- **No new rule.** Section 9.10 resolves a posting's deadline deterministically and has since 1.31 — `staffed`/`held` clears, `posted` breaks under Section 9.8, `withdrawn` leaves the board, *no roll and no Runtime discretion*. Not one word of it changes. What 1.44 adds is the **named moment at which the Runtime must consult it**, which is what was missing when three deadlines passed unsettled.
- **Section 14.3 gains `gatefall.board.deadline`.** Eligibility heading Section 9.10; settlement kind **`world_state_settlement`** (Decision 084, the engine's fourth and newest trigger-settlement kind); identity is the posting's board key; `cleared`, `broken`, and `withdrawn` block a second settlement.
- **Deltas:** `clock.advanced`, `supply.advanced`, `commitment.due`, `outreach.initiated`. The last settles the board **before** answering an inquiry and never generates in response to one — two questions inside one unadvanced span read the same board.
- **Not a System trigger.** Section 14.4 is unmoved: the System does not know what a public board is doing. No notification, no panel line, no `progression_audits` entry. A break across the city is news the Bearer hears the way anyone does, if at all.
- **Nothing moves at adoption.** No deadline re-derived, no staffing status changed, no board key minted or retired, no past scene reread. **The three passed deadlines are not settled by adopting this** — `GB-01` (midnight 2026-08-08), `GB-02` and `GB-03` (midnight 2026-08-09) stand exactly as they are; clearing them is ordinary Section 9.10 work in a normal session, tracked as design flag **F-002**.
- **Versions.** `system_state.profile_version` 1.43 → 1.44. Engine 0.2.0 and Data Model 0.1.5 unchanged. New migration record `worlds/gatefall/migrations/1.43_to_1.44.md`; chain 1.1 → 1.44, 43 edges.
- **Repository allocation.** `EVT-000268`; no entity, record, or relationship identifier minted. New high-water mark **EVT-000268**. Updated live targets: `090`, `100`, `160`, `170`, `180`, `system/ID_REGISTRY.md`, `system/WORLDS_AND_CAMPAIGNS.md`.

**2026-08-13, afternoon to evening — chasing the Marnie thread: Elias Ward, the wallet, the coffee stall, and the loading dock (`EVT-000323`-`EVT-000330`).**

- **The wallet and the sensor rig (`EVT-000323`).** At Elias Ward's shop, a Keen Sense read confirms an unmarked motion-sensor rig logs detected movement remotely. A legendary-success negotiation bundles it with a keyed document wallet for $800 total, breaking Elias's stated one-item-or-the-other policy. Auto-identify on banking (real d6 rolls) resolves the wallet C-Rank and the rig D-Rank — the same mechanism established at `EVT-000216`.
- **The lock (`EVT-000324`).** A first pick attempt fails; an Exploit Pattern read establishes the false-gate tell; a second attempt lands legendary. The wallet is empty save for a hidden lining seam carrying an unidentified maker's stamp. Elias places it as quiet, undocumented work; Walt Adamik tentatively — not certainly — links it to Marnie's own hand.
- **The Hidden quest pointer attaches (`EVT-000325`).** Reaching the coffee stall off Cermak satisfies `ENT-000190`'s proximity condition (non-daily slots 1/4→2/4, reward fixed D-Rank/150 XP). Keen Sense and Exploit Pattern read the stall owner's (`ENT-000191`, new) watch on a nearby dock. A direct approach naming Marnie lands partial success; invoking Walt Adamik's name with genuine sincerity lands a critical success. She confirms Marnie missed her standing Tuesday check-in for the first time ever, reports a professional, unbranded stranger asking pointed, artificer-literate questions about Marnie shortly before she went quiet, and flags the loading dock (`ENT-000192`, new). A follow-up press yields a physical description: plain suit, no tie, a tablet, no visible badge. `REL-000089` formed.
- **The dock (`EVT-000326`).** Keen Sense confirms an active, powered sensor and fresh tire tracks — no ambush. The Suppression Emitter (already owned, confirmed effective against mundane instrument-based detection) blinds it for a 20-minute window; a legendary lockpick opens the padlock. Inside: a work order referencing "the consultation" moved to an unnamed street, and a torn "CONSULT — TEMP" visitor-badge fragment, found by manual search after a Keen Sense read of the room's own residual texture fails outright. Three crates swept into the dimensional inventory under time pressure.
- **Extraction and the dagger (`EVT-000327`, `EVT-000328`).** An E-Rank Instant-Dungeon Key, bought as a planned extraction, opens on Overgrown Temple (🎲 d8 4) the moment Alexander clears the dock — solo clear, no damage, XP 660→970/1400; a redundant Flash Step rune sold (2,250 g), a second E-Rank key banked, 10 crystals sold (100 g); gold 1,841→3,691. Inside the sealed instance, the swept loot is examined properly: a finished dagger among it auto-identifies **S-Rank** — the same tier as the still-unclassified Dormant Core, confirming the missing artificer's work is exceptional and raising the stakes on her disappearance.
- **A new concealed-discovery record authored (`EVT-000329`).** "Who Runs the CONSULT Operation" (`ENT-000193`), anchored to the loading dock. No pointer attached this Event, per the mandatory time-separation rule.
- **Promotion-barrier settlement (`EVT-000330`).** Explicit `none` for `gatefall.skill_formation` — extensive Keen Sense/Exploit Pattern use, all already at Master/mastery-complete ceiling, produces no new candidate. Anchor advances 2026-08-13T15:30 → T18:30, same-day play. Health held at 200/200 throughout (no combat outside the trivial instant dungeon); Mana settled 98→90 across the session's casts, recovering toward full afterward.

**2026-08-13, evening close — the escape, Loomis Street, and telling Owen everything (`EVT-000332`-`EVT-000335`).**

- **Leaving the dock, and the second extraction (`EVT-000332`).** The Suppression Emitter is reactivated on the way out (🎲 d100 14, +1 step, effective 34, **failure**) — it blinds the dock's sensor as intended, but the sensor was never the risk: a plain-suited stranger matching the stall owner's CONSULT description rounds the corner on foot, tablet in hand. Ducking fully out of his sightline before triggering the second Instant-Dungeon Key [E-Rank] (🎲 d100 52, +1 step, effective 72, **success**) makes the extraction unwitnessed. Archetype **Beast Den** (🎲 d8 5): 9 common, 1 elite, and 2 boss-Rank alphas alongside the boss (×4 Rank Health each). Solo clear, no damage, resolved as montage. XP 970→1,230/1,400. Both boss drops redundant — a Flash Step [D-Rank] skill book (🎲 d100 92) and a Dagger Mastery [E-Rank] rune (🎲 d100 59) — sold at 2,250 g each; 12-crystal deposit sold for 120 g. A call to Walt Adamik relaying the full evening lands **partial success** (🎲 d100 42) — Loomis itself means nothing to him, but "CONSULT," temp badges, and someone who talks like they know the trade surface a two-month-old supplier rumor about a professional "processing operation" moving through the district. **He committed to asking that contact directly on 2026-08-14** — a real, dated, world-side thread that advances without Alexander acting.

His reaction is fear stated plainly as fact — that an operation organized enough to badge its own visitors is the kind of thing people get disappeared over — paired with explicit approval that he was told first, that night, unprompted. **The first concealed thread Alexander has volunteered in full before being asked.**

**2026-08-02 (real date) — checkpoint backfill and repair before `900_CHECKPOINT_0060`.**

The 2026-08-13 evening session's `/save` failed on an engine tooling defect (`F-010`, since fixed) and left three defects the failure had masked. All three are repaired here; none changes a played outcome.

- **`120_INVENTORY_AND_OWNERSHIP.md` had never been written for this session.** It stood at `EVT-000300` (2026-08-12) while the session moved $800 cash, gold 1,841→6,061, and eleven individually-tracked items including the S-Rank dagger — the ledger's own precedent covers exactly these transactions. The full cash and gold chain, the acquisitions, the disposals, and a provenance note recording that everything swept from the dock is **held, not owned**, are backfilled from the cited Events rather than re-derived from memory.
- **`EVT-000332`-`EVT-000334` were promoted into some ledgers but not others.** The character sheet carried `EVT-000332` while the registry high-water stood at `EVT-000331`, and `EVT-000333`/`EVT-000334` claimed participation records that `ENT-000125`, `ENT-000139`, and `ENT-000147` did not carry — three Decision 085 "claimed and not made" failures plus a missing Section 7.1 settlement. Entity blocks updated, `EVT-000335` written, registry advanced. **`130`'s Closed Channel row cited `EVT-000333` where the telling is `EVT-000334`** — corrected.
- **The character sheet's gold was stale at 8,311**, recorded before the D-Rank key purchase that closed `EVT-000332`. Corrected to **6,061**, and the key added to `shop_holdings`.
- **Two Section 8.4.3/8.4.5 violations in the session's own new canon.** `ENT-000190`'s world-ledger record stored `reward_rank_at_attachment: D-Rank`; a concealed-discovery record **never stores a reward** — reward is fixed from System Rank at attachment — so the field is removed. And `trigger_telemetry.concealed_records_available` read **16** where the ledgers hold **15**: authoring `ENT-000193` added one to the available pool, but attaching `ENT-000190`'s pointer removed one in the same checkpoint, and only the addition had been counted. Corrected to 15, `as_of` advanced to the 20:30 anchor.
- **Three skills' rendered prose disagreed with their stored counters.** Section 7.4 makes the prose a *rendering* of the typed counters, so the counters govern: **Keen Sense 28→31** and **Exploit Pattern 24→26** were rendered behind the uses this session actually settled, and **Rupture 53→49** was rendered ahead of its own counter. Only the prose changed; no counter, multiplier, or mastery tier was touched. This is what the checkpoint contract's skill-counter gate caught — the sheet claimed two dungeon clears' worth of progression against a frozen rendered total.

- **The quest was tracking less than the fiction had established.** `objective_counters` stopped at the coffee-stall account and recorded nothing of the evening after it, and mis-cited that account to `EVT-000326` when the stall owner scene is `EVT-000325`.

- **Reward deliberately unchanged, and the reasoning is recorded.** Section 8.4.3 fixes a Hidden quest's reward from the Bearer's Rank at attachment — D-Rank, 150 XP, set at `EVT-000325` — and later events do not reprice it. **The S-Rank dagger is loot from the dock sweep, not a quest reward**; it satisfied no condition of this quest, and a reward that grew with incidental loot would stop being the fixed promise 8.4.3 defines.

- **Repository allocation.** `EVT-000337`; no entity, record, or relationship identifier minted. New high-water mark **EVT-000337**. Updated live targets: `100`, `160`, `170`, `180`, `system/ID_REGISTRY.md`.

**2026-08-02 (real date) — `F-011` settled toward the ascension (`EVT-000336`).**

- **Owner ruling.** Where a promoted Event's description narrates a mastery ascension, that ascension is canon and its counters are brought up to it. **Twin Fang and Exploit Pattern** advance to **Master** (`mastery_level` 4→5, progress 2→0), ratifying `EVT-000303`. **Keen Sense** likewise (progress 3→0, its `-3` reset reflecting a threshold already reached in an earlier Event). Written forward as a new Event rather than by editing promoted canon, on the `EVT-000237` precedent.
- **Rupture excluded, corrected the other way.** `EVT-000304` makes the same ascension claim, but Rupture's counter arithmetic gives Expert (baseline 5, `-2` at `EVT-000162`'s Rank ascension, `+1` at `EVT-000238`), its rendered numbers are **Expert's** (×2.45, Mana 9 — D-Rank Master is ×2.60), and its progress of 1 makes the `-2` reset impossible. Its star-label is corrected **Master → Expert**, progress 1/3.
- **Nothing a player sees changed.** Three skills' typed state caught up to what the sheet has rendered and play has resolved all along; one label came down to match its own numbers. No multiplier, Mana cost, or scope moved.
- **Damage previews repinned, unrelated to the ruling.** `tools/test_gatefall_quest_contract.ps1` pinned its `/system` previews at effective Strength 51 and they had been stale since the Level 14 advance to 62: main-hand 65→**77**, off-hand 61→**72**, Twin Fang follow-ups 88→**116** and 94→**123**. Rupture's 61 is unchanged. The test's `mastery progress N/3` assertion also could not express a ceiling render, making it unsatisfiable for any skill that actually reached Master; it now accepts `mastery progress complete` at Master, and the three ascending skills were normalized to that house style.
- **Repository allocation.** `EVT-000336`; no entity, record, or relationship identifier minted. New high-water mark **EVT-000336**. Updated live targets: `100`, `160`, `170`, `180`, `system/ID_REGISTRY.md`.

- **Superseded, recorded at `900_CHECKPOINT_0060` as still open (`F-011`).** `EVT-000303`'s promoted description states Twin Fang and Exploit Pattern each ascended **Expert→Master** in the 2026-08-12 Beast Den clear, but that Event's `counter_deltas` carry only `successful_uses` and `qualifying_scenes_total` — no `mastery_level` or `mastery_progress` increment — so both skills' stored `mastery_level` still reads **4 (Expert)** with `mastery_progress` 2/3 while their prose renders **Master**. The same shape affects Keen Sense (stored Expert at 3/3, rendered Master). Resolving it either advances three multipliers or retracts three promoted ascension statements, which is an owner ruling on mechanics rather than a checkpoint repair, and the discrepancy already sits inside `900_CHECKPOINT_0059`. `tools/test_gatefall_quest_contract.ps1` fails on it and is the standing evidence; it is not one of the checkpoint's own gates.
